- 2026-06-12 — Wave 420（gatewayruntime 纯 helper 离线分支覆盖）`gatewayruntime` 包整体覆盖率最低（44.6%），其网络/DB/轮询路径受 live Telegram/webhook 与 SQLite 门控，但一批纯 helper 可离线断言且此前 0%/低覆盖：为 `stringValue`/`boolValue`/`intFromAny`/`firstNonEmptyString`/`splitList`/`stringSlice`/`platformConfigFromMap`/`upsertPlatform`/`gatewayMessageFromPayload`/`rateLimiter.Allow` 补 focused 测试（`helpers_test.go`）。要点：(1) 值强转 helper 的类型守卫（非 string/bool/number 返回零值，JSON number 以 float64 解码）；(2) `firstNonEmptyString`/`splitList` 的 trim+跳空语义；(3) `stringSlice` 仅接受 `[]any` 且逐项 trim/跳空；(4) `platformConfigFromMap` 的 `token` 优先于 `botToken`（`firstNonEmptyString` 回退）、`allowedUsers` 解析；(5) `upsertPlatform` 按 platform key 覆盖或追加；(6) `gatewayMessageFromPayload` 仅对 `type=="message"` 提取 id/role/contentText，非 message 或无法 marshal（如 channel）返回三空串；(7) `rateLimiter.Allow` 的每分钟滑窗限流与 per-key 独立预算。包覆盖率 44.6% → 58.1%。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。本轮仅为既有纯 helper 补离线测试安全网，不改变任何运行时行为；gateway 的 live Telegram 轮询/发送、webhook、DB 映射 CRUD 等仍受环境门控。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 421（mcpcompat MCP 导入格式检测离线分支覆盖 + 一处分歧记录）`mcpcompat` 包此前仅 `TestImportServersPersistsSettings` 覆盖 `mcpServers` 包装路径，MCP 配置导入的格式检测纯 helper 的多数分支未测。为 `normalizeImportServerMap`/`isSingleServerConfig`/`deriveImportServerName`/`detectTransport`/`importMap` 补 focused 测试（`import_format_test.go`），逐条对照 TS `server/routes/mcp.ts` 的 `/servers/import` 归一化逻辑：(1) `normalizeImportServerMap` 接受 `{mcpServers:{}}`/`{servers:{}}`/单服务器配置对象（JetBrains 风格 `{type,url}`）/裸 `{name:{}}` 服务器映射，其余返回 400；(2) `isSingleServerConfig` 的 url/command/transport(stdio|sse|streamable-http)/type 四类检测分支；(3) `deriveImportServerName` 从 url host 派生（localhost 带端口特例 → `localhost-{port}`）、command basename（含 Windows 反斜杠路径、尾斜杠）、无可用字段回退 `imported-server`、非法 url 回退；(4) `detectTransport` 显式 transport/type 优先、url-without-command → streamable-http、默认 stdio；(5) `importMap` 过滤非对象条目、非 map 输入返回 nil。包覆盖率 68.5% → 71.3%。**记录一处已知次要分歧（未改运行时）**：TS 裸服务器映射判定用 `keys.every(k => typeof json[k]==="object")`，任一根键值非对象即返回 400「Unrecognized format」；Go 的 `normalizeImportServerMap` 末路径用 `importMap` 会**过滤**非对象键并在剩余 ≥1 个对象时成功。故对混合根（如 `{"good":{...},"label":"x"}` 且无 mcpServers/servers、非单服务器配置）TS 拒绝而 Go 导入 `good`。鉴于 TS 侧依赖 JS `typeof null==="object"`、数组亦为对象等 quirk，强行在 Go 复刻易引入新 bug 且实际仅影响导入畸形配置，按诚实低风险原则**记录分歧而非静默改 Go 行为**；测试未对该混合场景断言「一致」（仅断言全非对象根两侧均拒绝，属 parity-correct）。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。本轮仅补离线测试并记录一处分歧，不改变任何运行时行为。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 422（userprefs 自定义命令校验离线分支覆盖 + 一处长度计量分歧记录）`userprefs` 包的自定义命令输入校验 helper（`validateCommandParams`/`validateCommandModelOverride`/`optionalIntRangeField`/`optionalEnumField`）此前仅经 HTTP handler 间接触达、分支覆盖低。补 focused 测试（`command_validation_test.go`），逐条对照 TS Zod schema `server/lib/validators/common.ts`：(1) params 可选数组、至多 20 项，每项 name 必填(1..50)、description 可选(<=500)、required 布尔、defaultValue 可选(<=1000)，覆盖缺失/越界/类型错误各分支；(2) modelOverride 可选对象，model 必填(1..100)、mode 枚举(temporary|permanent)；(3) `optionalIntRangeField` 整数范围校验 + JSON float64→int 原地改写、非整数/越界拒绝；(4) `optionalEnumField` 允许值/缺省/非法值/非字符串分支。包覆盖率 67.2% → 70.2%。**记录一处已知长度计量分歧（未改运行时）**：Go 校验用 `len(text)`（UTF-8 字节数），TS Zod `z.string().max(n)` 计 UTF-16 code unit（≈字符数）。对纯 ASCII 两者等价（测试用例均为 ASCII，断言 parity-correct），但对多字节输入（如 CJK name「城市」=2 字符/6 字节）Go 的字节上限更严，可能拒绝 TS 接受的合法输入。此为既有 Go 实现行为而非本轮引入；鉴于改为 rune/UTF-16 计量属运行时行为变更且影响面需评估（多处 helper 共用），按诚实低风险原则记录分歧待后续统一决策，本轮不改运行时。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。本轮仅补离线测试并记录一处分歧，不改变任何运行时行为。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 423（修复 userprefs 字符串长度校验的字节/字符计量 parity bug）落实 Wave 422 记录的长度计量分歧：调研发现 go_backend 其余包（`projects`/`chapters`/`settings`/`search`）的 Zod-`.max()`-镜像长度校验**已统一用 `utf8.RuneCountInString`**（字符数），而 `userprefs` 的命令/偏好校验 helper 仍误用 `len()`（UTF-8 字节数），属不一致的离群实现——对 CJK 输入会错误拒绝 TS 接受的合法值（如 50 个中文字符的命令 name = 150 字节，被 Go 的 `max 50` 字节上限误拒）。将 `userprefs` 五处语义长度校验由 `len(text)` 改为 `utf8.RuneCountInString(text)`：`validateStringPreference`(min/max)、`requiredStringField`(min/max)、`optionalStringField`(min/max)、偏好 secret 字段 `<=500`、`validateGatewayStringArray` 每项 `<=100`。错误消息本就写「characters」，改后语义准确。`maskSecret` 的 `value[len(value)-4:]` 是显示遮罩的字节切片操作（非语义长度限制，且有既有测试锁定），保持不变。新增 CJK 回归测试：50/1000 个中文字符的 name/defaultValue/model 现可通过、51/101 字符仍被拒。本修复严格朝 TS 参考**放宽**（不会拒绝 TS 接受的任何输入），且使 `userprefs` 与 go_backend 既有约定一致，低风险。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。注：`mcpcompat`/`favorites` 等包仍有少量 `len()` 语义长度校验（同类分歧），留作后续按相同模式逐包修复的诚实待办。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 424（续修 favorites/mcpcompat 字符串长度校验字节/字符 parity bug）延续 Wave 423 工作线，把同类字节/字符计量 bug 在 `favorites` 与 `mcpcompat` 包修复。逐一对照 TS 参考确认均为 Zod-`.max()`-镜像的语义字符限制：favorites（`server/lib/validators/settings.ts`）path `.max(4096)`、label `.max(200)`；mcpcompat（`server/routes/mcp.ts` 内联 schema）name/toolName `.max(200)`、command/cwd/args 项 `.max(500)`、url/env 值 `.max(2000)`、env 键 `.max(200)`。修复点：(1) favorites `requiredStringField`(min)、`optionalStringValue`(max) 由 `len()` 改 `utf8.RuneCountInString`；(2) mcpcompat `validateOptionalString`(min/max)、`stringArrayValueStrict`(每项 max)、`stringObjectValueStrict`(键/值 max) 同改。新增两个 CJK 回归测试（`favorites/rune_length_test.go`、`mcpcompat/rune_length_test.go`）：200 中文字符 label、4096 中文字符 path、500 中文字符 command/arg、200 中文字符 env 键、2000 中文字符 env 值现可通过，超 1 字符仍被拒。**保持不变**：mcpcompat `narratorContextSummaryText` 的 `len(text)>500` + `text[:500]` 是上下文摘要的显示截断（字节切片，输出预算而非输入校验），非 Zod 镜像，故不动（改 rune 需同步改切片，属另一类关注点）。本修复严格朝 TS 参考**放宽**且与 go_backend 既有 `utf8.RuneCountInString` 约定一致，低风险。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。注：`misccompat` 等包仍有 `len()` 语义长度校验（同类分歧），留作后续同模式修复的诚实待办。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 425（续修 misccompat 字符串长度校验字节/字符 parity bug）延续 Wave 423/424 工作线，修复 `misccompat` 包同类字节/字符计量 bug。逐一对照 TS schema 确认均为 Zod-`.max()`/`.min()`-镜像的语义字符限制：hooks（`server/lib/validators/hooks.ts`）projectId `.max(100)`、matcher `.max(200)`、command `.max(10000)`、url `.max(2000)`、headers 值 `.max(2000)`；workspaces（`validators/workspaces.ts`）title `.max(200)`、tree `.min(2).max(50000)`；volume snapshots（`validators/containers.ts`）serviceName `.max(200)`、containerPath `.max(1000)`、name `.max(200)`、description `.max(2000)`。修复点（全部 `len()` → `utf8.RuneCountInString`）：`decodeWorkspaceBody`(title/tree min+max)、`decodeCreateHookBody`(projectId/matcher/command/url)、hook patch 解析(matcher/command/url)、`stringMapField`(headers 值)、`createVolumeSnapshot`(serviceName/name)、`updateVolumeSnapshot`(name)、`validateVolumeSnapshotDescription`、`volume_snapshot.go` containerPath。新增 4 个 CJK 回归测试（`misccompat/rune_length_test.go`）：200 中文字符的 header 值上限、2000 字符 description、200 字符 hook matcher、200 字符 workspace title 现可通过，超 1 字符仍被拒。**保持不变**：`validateSnapshotID`（仅允许 ASCII `[a-zA-Z0-9_-]`，byte==rune 无 bug）；`narratorContextSummaryText` 显示截断（输出预算字节切片）。本修复严格朝 TS **放宽**且与 go_backend 既有 `utf8.RuneCountInString` 约定一致，低风险。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。**剩余同类分歧（下一轮诚实待办）**：repo 扫描显示 `narrators/handlers.go`(5131/5317/5325)、`simplecontent/handlers.go`(831)、`ws/hub.go`(2556/2626)、`systemroutes/handlers.go`(4029/4041 等) 仍有 `len()` 语义长度校验候选，需逐一对照 TS schema 区分语义字符限制（应修）与字节预算（应留）后再处理。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 426（收尾 narrators/simplecontent/ws 字符串长度校验字节/字符 parity bug + 全局清零确认）完成 Wave 423-425 工作线，修复剩余包的同类 bug 并全局确认清零。逐一对照 TS schema 确认为 Zod 字符限制后修复（全部 `len()` → `utf8.RuneCountInString`）：(1) narrators（`validators/common.ts`）`validateNarratorRuleFields` 的 path `.max(4096)`、pattern `.max(200)`、denyPrompt `.max(2000)`，及 `updateBufferedMessageSchema` text `.max(100000)`；(2) simplecontent（`validators/skills.ts`）`validateProjectSkillBounds` 的 name `.max(100)`、description `.max(2000)`、content `.max(200000)`；(3) ws（`validators/terminals.ts`+`narratorWsMessageSchema`）terminal create/rename name `.max(100)`、narrator buffer_message/update_buffer text `.max(100000)`（共 4 处）。新增两个 CJK 回归测试（`simplecontent/skill_bounds_rune_test.go`、`narrators/rule_fields_rune_test.go`）：100/2000/200000 中文字符的 skill name/description/content、4096 字符 path、200 字符 pattern、2000 字符 denyPrompt 现可通过，超 1 字符仍被拒。**经审查确认保持 `len()` 不变的字节预算/截断点**（非 Zod 字符限制，正确）：narrators/ws/mcpcompat/systemroutes 的上下文摘要与 nug usage 显示截断（`text[:N]` 字节切片）、gateway webhook text「exceeds 65536 **bytes**」、telegram 4096 字节分片、`validateSnapshotID`/benchmark/update 的 ASCII 文件名或纯 ASCII 字段。**全局清零确认**：`grep` 全 repo 已无「返回错误且消息含 characters/too long/at most」的 `len()` 语义长度校验残留。本工作线（Wave 423→426）系统性修复了 6 个包（userprefs/favorites/mcpcompat/misccompat/narrators/simplecontent/ws）共 30+ 处面向 CJK 用户的字节/字符计量 parity bug，每处均附 TS 对照、CJK 回归测试、并诚实区分应修（语义字符限制）vs 应留（字节预算/截断/ASCII）。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。本修复严格朝 TS **放宽**且与 go_backend 既有 `utf8.RuneCountInString` 约定一致，低风险，不改变 ASCII 输入行为。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 427（修复 narrator 规则/cwd 的 Zod `.trim()` 存储 parity bug）发现并修复一类新的真实 bug：TS `z.string().trim()` 不仅校验**去空白后**的值，还**存储去空白后的值**；Go 此前校验 trim 后的值却存储/回显原始（未 trim）值，导致带首尾空白的 path/pattern/cwd 在 Go 存成含杂散空白的字符串，规则匹配行为与 TS 不一致。逐一对照 TS 确认修复：(1) `createRule`（narrator whitelist/blacklist dirs+cmds）的 primary 字段 path（`createWhitelistDirSchema`/`createBlacklistDirSchema` `.trim().min(1).max(4096)`）与 pattern（`createWhitelistCmdSchema`/`createBlacklistCmdSchema` `.trim().min(1).max(200)`）在校验后、INSERT 前 `strings.TrimSpace` 再持久化（secondary 的 denyPrompt TS 无 `.trim()`，保持不变）；(2) `updateCwd`（`updateNarratorCwdSchema` `cwd: .trim().min(1).max(4096)`）改为存储/回显 `trimmedCwd` 而非 `body.Cwd`。顺带修复同处遗漏的字节/字符长度 bug：`updateCwd` 的 `len(trimmedCwd)>4096`、`validateCreateRequest` 的 systemPrompt `len()>10000` 与 cwd `len()>4096`（TS `createNarratorSchema` systemPrompt `.max(10000)`、cwd `.max(4096)`，均字符数）改为 `utf8.RuneCountInString`（create cwd 无 `.trim()`，故仅改计量不改存储）。新增回归测试（`narrators/rule_trim_test.go`）：whitelist dir path `"  /home/project/src  "` 与 cmd pattern `"  git status  "` 存储为 trim 后值、纯空白被拒；PATCH cwd `"  /home/project  "` 回显并存储 trim 后值、纯空白被拒。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。本修复使 Go 存储语义与 TS `.trim()` 严格一致，修正了规则匹配/cwd 解析的潜在正确性问题，低风险。注：后续可审查其余 `z.string().trim()` schema（如有更多落库路径）确认同类存储一致性。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 428（修复 settings 权限规则的 Zod `.trim()` 存储 parity bug + 完成 `.trim()` 全审查）延续 Wave 427，审查 TS 全部 `z.string().trim()` schema（`common.ts` 的 whitelist/blacklist dir `path` + command whitelist/blacklist `pattern`，`narrators.ts` 已在 W427 处理）并修复 settings 落库路径的同类 bug。`settingsRequiredTrimmedString`（被 `validateAgentPathPolicyRules`/`validateAgentCommandPolicyRules` 用于校验 agent.whitelistDirs/blacklistDirs path 与 commandWhitelist/commandBlacklist pattern）此前 `strings.TrimSpace` 仅用于校验、未写回，导致 PATCH /api/settings 把带首尾空白的 path/pattern 原样持久化，与 TS `.trim()` 存储不一致。修复：校验通过后 `item[key] = text` 写回 trim 后的值（map 引用，随 `h.Store.Patch(patch)` 持久化）。顺带把同处及相邻两个 helper 的字节/字符长度 bug 修正：`settingsRequiredTrimmedString`、`validateSettingsStringFieldBounds`（modelAggregations id/name `.max(20)`/`.max(100)`）、`settingsOptionalStringMaxField`（note/denyPrompt `.max(200)`/`.max(2000)`）的 `len()` → `utf8.RuneCountInString`（后两者 TS 无 `.trim()`，仅改计量不改存储；`settingsRequiredStringField` 的 `len()<minLen` 仅判空，byte==rune 无需改）。新增回归测试（`settings/policy_trim_test.go`）：PATCH 含首尾空白的 whitelistDir path `"  /home/project/src  "` 与 commandWhitelist pattern `"  git status  "`，断言 `store.Raw()` 中持久化值已 trim。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。**`.trim()` 存储 parity 工作线（Wave 427-428）完成**：TS 全部 `z.string().trim()` 落库路径（narrator dir/cmd 规则、narrator cwd、settings 全局/项目级权限规则）的 Go 存储已与 TS 一致（校验+存储均用 trim 后值）；非落库的纯校验 `.trim()`（如仅用于匹配的运行时检查）不涉及存储一致性。低风险，不改变无空白输入的行为。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 429（修复 auth 密码/资料字段的字节/字符长度 parity bug）在审查 TS Zod transform parity 时，于 auth 域发现并修复一批此前遗漏的字节/字符长度 bug——这是安全敏感路径，CJK 密码被错误拒绝。逐一对照 TS（`server/lib/validators/auth.ts`）确认为字符限制：(1) `registerSchema`/admin update password `z.string().min(8).max(128)`——`auth/service.go` Register 与 `systemroutes/handlers.go` admin 用户更新的 `len(password)` → `utf8.RuneCountInString`（8 个中文字符密码=24 字节，TS 接受但 Go 字节校验下 3 字符密码=9 字节会被误判为合法、129 字符密码会被误拒）；(2) `updateProfileSchema` gitUsername `.max(100)`、gitEmail `.max(254)`——`auth/service.go` UpdateProfile 的 `len()` → `utf8.RuneCountInString`。**保持 `len()` 不变（正确）**：username（`registerSchema`/`adminUpdateUserSchema` 的 `regex(^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$)` 约束为 ASCII，byte==rune）、language（`.min(1).max(10)` 实为 BCP-47 ASCII 标签）、password 的 `<minLen` 判空在 minLen=1 时 byte==rune（但 password minLen=8 已改）。新增回归测试（`auth/length_rune_test.go`）：3 个中文字符密码被拒、129 字符被拒、8 字符（24 字节）注册成功；100 字符中文 gitUsername 存储成功、101 字符被拒。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。本修复严格朝 TS **放宽**且与既有 `utf8.RuneCountInString` 约定一致，修正了中文用户无法使用 CJK 密码/资料的真实可见 bug，低风险。注：本轮源于 Zod transform parity 审查（`.coerce`/`.transform` 已确认 git log/diff 查询参数处理与 TS 一致，无实质分歧；email/username 无 `.toLowerCase()` 存储归一化分歧）。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 430（字节/字符长度二次全扫描补漏 + 修复 recentTab title 200/1000 值不匹配）对 go_backend 做更彻底的 `len() > N` 二次扫描（含错误消息跨行、内联 handler 校验，非仅 helper），补修前几轮漏掉的语义字符限制并发现一处独立的限值 bug。修复（`len()` → `utf8.RuneCountInString`，逐一对照 TS 确认字符限制）：(1) explorations（`validators/explorations.ts`）create/update 的 title `.max(200)`、description `.max(2000)`、branch title/description（共 6 处内联）；(2) terminals（`createTerminalSchema`）name `.max(100)`；(3) simplecontent 全局 skill create 内联 name/description/content（`.max(100)`/`.max(2000)`/`.max(200000)`，与 W426 的 `validateProjectSkillBounds` 同源不同路径）；(4) narrators（`validators/narrators.ts`）fork title `.max(200)`（2 处）、ask question `.max(10000)`、rename title `.max(200)`、model `.max(200)`、sourceId `.max(120)`；(5) misccompat ruler merge message（`validators/ruler.ts` `.max(500)`）。**独立限值 bug（非字节/字符）**：recentTab title/subtitle Go 此前硬编码上限 200，但 TS `recentTabSchema` 用 `RECENT_TAB_TEXT_MAX_CHARS = 1000`——Go 会错误拒绝 201..1000 字符的标签标题；改为 1000 与 TS 一致。**保持 `len()` 不变（正确，经逐一核对）**：各类 `text[:N]` 显示/存储截断（narrators service.go 4016/5593/7879、handlers.go 1152/2321 firstLine title 截断）、数组计数（MessageIDs/Branches/Positions `> N items`）、ASCII 标识符（recentTab id/key、username pattern、language BCP-47 标签、snapshotID）、字节预算（webhook 65536 bytes、telegram 4096）。新增回归测试：explorations 200 字符 CJK title/branch 接受（`handlers_test.go`）、recentTab 1000 字符 title 接受/1001 拒（`handlers_json_test.go`）。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。本轮严格朝 TS **放宽/对齐**，修正中文用户在 exploration/terminal/skill/narrator/ruler 命名及 recentTab 长标题上的真实可见 bug，低风险。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 431（字节/字符长度三次扫描：chapters/projects/gitroutes/settings/narrators 补漏）继续二次/三次扫描，在 handler 层（区别于已用 RuneCount 的 service 层）补修一批 `len()` 语义字符限制。逐一对照 TS schema 确认字符限制后 `len()` → `utf8.RuneCountInString`：(1) chapters handlers（`validators/chapters.ts`）create/update title `.max(200)`+description `.max(2000)`、split newFork title/description、merge message `.max(500)`（共 9 处；注 chapters service.go 1800-1860 已用 RuneCount，本轮补的是 handlers.go）；(2) projects handlers（`validators/projects.ts`）create name/cloneUsername/clonePassword `.max(200)`+description `.max(2000)`（service.go 已用 RuneCount，补 handlers.go 4 处）；(3) gitroutes（`validators/git.ts`）commit message `.max(500)`、stash message `.max(200)`、reset target `.max(100)`；(4) settings（`routes/settings.ts`）server.host/vnet.udp.host `.max(255)`、agent.defaultSystemPrompt `.max(50000)`；(5) narrators handlers segment-compact summary `.max(100000)`、content `.max(100000)`、draft text `.max(100000)`。**核对保持 `len()` 不变**：chapters/narrators/systemroutes/agentbasic 的 `text[:N]` 截断（service.go 949/1008、handlers.go 1096、webfetch_dom）、数组计数（Forks>10、MessageIDs 等）、ASCII 字段（username pattern、git target/branch 实际 ASCII 但仍按 TS 改为 rune 以严格对齐）。同时确认数组 `.max()` 计数限制（tierOrder max 6、messageIds 1..500）Go 侧已正确enforced。新增回归测试（`chapters/create_title_rune_test.go`）：200 字符 CJK 章节标题通过、201 拒。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。**字节/字符长度 parity 工作线至此覆盖 13+ 包**：全局 `grep` 已无「返回错误且消息含 characters/too long/at most」的 `len()` 语义长度校验残留（仅余正确的截断/数组计数/ASCII-min 判空）。本轮严格朝 TS 放宽/对齐，低风险，不改变 ASCII 输入行为。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 432（数值/枚举边界 parity 审计 — 负结果，无代码改动）在字节/字符长度工作线清零后，审查下一维度：TS Zod 数值 `.min/.max/.int/.finite` 与 `z.enum` 取值集合。结论是**当前无可修复的真实 gap**，据实记录以免后续重复调研：(1) **`z.number().finite()`（graph positions axisOffset/crossOffset/panelWidth/panelHeight）不可达**——曾假设 Go 会把 `1e400` 解码为 ±Inf 而漏过 finite 校验，遂加了 `isFiniteFloat` 守卫与回归测试；但实测 Go `encoding/json` 在解码越界字面量（如 `1e400`）到 `float64` 时**直接报 unmarshal 错误**（在 `DecodeJSON` 阶段即 400 拒绝），且 JSON 无 NaN/Inf 字面量，故该 finite 校验在普通解码路径下是死代码。已**完整还原**该改动（含 import/helper/test），未留无法触达的伪修复；graph 包 `git diff` 干净。(2) **整数/范围边界已正确**：hook timeout `1..600`（create+update 双路径）、containerPort `1..65535`、git log limit/skip `1..200`/`>=0`、modelAggregation tierOrder `max 6`、messageIds `1..500` 等 Go 侧均已 enforced；`intField` 正确镜像 `.int()`（拒绝非整数 float64）。(3) **`z.coerce.number()`**（git 查询参数）已于 Wave 已确认与 TS 一致。本轮无代码改动（净零 diff），价值在于排除了一个看似存在实则不可达的 gap 并记录数值/枚举维度的核对结论。教训：移植 parity 修复前须先验证目标运行时（Go encoding/json）的真实行为，而非假设其与 JS 一致——否则会写出死代码伪修复。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 433（修复 split inheritMode 的 Zod `.default()` 缺失 + 纠正一个把 bug 当预期的测试）审查 Zod `.default()` 维度，发现并修复一处真实 bug：TS `splitChapterSchema.newFork.inheritMode` 是 `z.enum(["full","compressed","fresh"]).default("full")`——省略该字段时 TS 默认 "full" 并放行；但 Go split handler（`chapters/handlers.go`）此前对空/省略的 inheritMode **返回 400**。修复：校验前 `if inheritMode == "" { inheritMode = "full" }`，与 TS 默认一致。**同时纠正一个错误的既有测试**：`TestSplitRouteValidatesInputBeforeMutation` 中名为 "inheritMode required" 的用例断言省略 inheritMode 应得 400——这恰恰把 bug 固化为预期行为。已删除该错误用例，并新增 `TestSplitDefaultsOmittedInheritModeToFull` 验证省略 inheritMode 现在默认 "full"（通过校验后到达 `SplitAtCommit` 的 services 守卫，而非 inheritMode 枚举错误）。**核对确认其余 `.default()` 已正确**：fork role 空→"branch"、fork/batch-fork inheritMode 空→"full"（service `normalizeInheritMode`，且对真正非法值如 "summary" 仍返回 400）、ruler strategy 空→"merge"、project flowMode 非"ruler"→"classic"、hook timeout 默认 30 等均与 TS `.default()` 一致。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。本修复使 Go 在省略 inheritMode 时与 TS 同样放行（朝 TS 放宽），修正了依赖该默认值的客户端在 Go 下被误拒的真实 bug，低风险。教训：补 parity 时要警惕"把现有 Go 行为当作正确基线"的测试——它们可能固化了与 TS 的偏差。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 434（`.default()` 剩余站点 + `.nullable().optional()` 维度审计 — 负结果，无代码改动）继 Wave 433 修复 split inheritMode 后，把 `.default()` 与 `.nullable().optional()` 两个维度审完，结论是**其余站点均已正确**，据实记录以免重复调研：(1) **`.default()` 剩余站点正确**：MCP create（`routes/mcp.ts` name 空省略→"Untitled"、transport→"stdio"、enabled→true，经 `normalizeServerConfig` 镜像，且对空字符串 name/transport 同样按 `.min(1)`/enum 拒绝）、usage-history granularity（非 hour/month→"day"）、ruler strategy→"merge"、project flowMode→"classic"、fork role→"branch"/inheritMode→"full"、hook timeout→30 等均与 TS `.default()` 一致。(2) **`.nullable().optional()` 维度正确**：chapter update color/groupLabel/containerConfig、project update startupScript/copyFiles/proxyDomain、hook update command/url/headers 均通过 `map[string]any` 解码（`DecodeLooseJSON`）保留「null（清空）vs 省略（不变）」的区分；service 层迭代 fields map，存在键即 `SET col = ?`（JSON null→`nil`→SQL NULL 清空），缺键则不进 SET 子句（保持不变），与 TS Zod `.nullable().optional()` 语义一致；且对 null 值跳过 max 长度校验（`ok && value != nil`），对非 null 非法类型/超长仍拒绝。本轮无代码改动（净零 diff），价值在于确认 `.default()`/`.nullable()` 两维度无残留 gap。**Zod transform/validation parity 审计总结**：字节/字符长度（清零）、`.trim()` 存储（清零）、`.default()`（修 1 个 bug，余正确）、数值/`.int()`/`.finite()`（无 gap）、`z.enum`/数组 `.max()` 计数（正确）、`.nullable().optional()` 空值语义（正确）——主要 Zod 维度均已系统审计完毕。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 435（错误响应形状/状态码维度审计 + WriteError 跨栈契约回归测试）转入 Zod 之外的 parity 维度：错误响应体形状与 HTTP 状态码映射。审计结论：(1) **错误体形状已是兼容超集**——TS `app.onError`（`server/app.ts:258`）序列化为 `{error: msg, code}` 两字段；Go `httpx.WriteError` 序列化为 `{error, message, code}` 三字段（多一个 `message`，值=error）。前端 `readFetchError`（`frontend/lib/api/client.ts:47`）按 `["reason","message","error"]` 顺序取文本、按 `code` 取码——TS 下无 message 走 `error` 兜底，Go 下取 `message`（=error，文本相同）。两端渲染一致，Go 的多余 `message` 字段无害且实为前端首选，属良性兼容设计，**不改运行时**。(2) **状态码映射**：NOT_FOUND=404、VALIDATION_ERROR=400、PODMAN_NOT_FOUND=422、CONTAINER_ERROR/BAD_GATEWAY=502、GIT_AUTH=401 等 Go 侧一致；git 路由的 `goReturns400TSParseQuirk`（Go 干净 400 vs TS Zod parse 返回 422/500）已是 matrix 既录的有意分歧（TS 自身在该路径也不一致）。本轮虽无运行时改动，但为此前**完全未被直接测试**的 `httpx.WriteError` 输出形状补 focused 回归测试（`httpx/response_test.go` `TestWriteErrorShapeMatchesCrossStackContract`，3 子用例）：锁定 AppError→`{error,message,code}`+message==error+状态码映射、非 AppError→500 `{error:"Internal server error"}`、FeatureDisabled→完整 fallback 信封（ok/code/feature/supported/fallback/reason/error/message + supported=false/fallback=true）。此前该跨栈错误契约仅隐式依赖，现升级为显式锁定，防止未来误改前端依赖的字段集。`gofmt`、`GOPROXY=off go build ./...`、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`git diff --check` 干净。本轮以测试锁定既有正确契约，不改运行时。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 436（enum 取值集合 + gateway 平台配置完整性审计 — 负结果，无代码改动）审计 `z.enum` 取值集合与 gateway 平台配置的字段级完整性，结论是**均已正确且已被测试覆盖**：(1) **enum 取值集合一致**：逐一核对 TS 全部 `z.enum`（chapter status/role、inheritMode、merge strategy、hook event/type、reasoningEffort、gateway dmPolicy/groupPolicy、layout 等）与 Go 对应 case 集合一致。**特别核对一处易错点**：recentTab `type` 在 TS 有两个不同 enum——upsert（`recentTabSchema`，无 "session"）vs remove（`removeRecentTabSchema`，含 "session"）；Go 正确用两个独立校验器（`validateUpsertRecentTab` 无 session / `validateRemoveRecentTab` 含 session）精确镜像，无张冠李戴。(2) **gateway 平台配置字段级完整 parity**：`validateGatewayPlatformConfig` 与 TS `gatewayPlatformConfigSchema` 字段逐一对应——platform 7 值枚举（telegram/discord/slack/feishu/webhook/weixin/qqbot）、enabled 必填、token/botToken/appToken/appSecret/secret/clientSecret(≤500)、appId/accountId(≤200)、baseUrl(≤500)、dmPolicy/groupPolicy 枚举(open/allowlist/disabled)、markdownSupport/sandbox 布尔、allowedGroups/allowedUsers(≤50 项/≤100 字符/项)、stt 对象，无缺漏/多余字段。且既有测试 `TestPatchUserPreferencesRejectsInvalidTypedFields` 已覆盖未知 platform（"unknown"）与缺 enabled 的拒绝。本轮无代码改动（净零 diff），价值在于确认 enum/gateway 维度无残留 gap。**附记 harness 覆盖待办（需双后端环境）**：对照 Go 注册路由前缀与 `ts-go-api-contract-diff.ts` 采样集，发现 `/api/usage-history`、`/api/reviews`、`/api/terminals`、`/api/project-db` 等前缀尚未被跨后端 harness 采样（`/api/exploration-groups` 系 Go-only 有意不采样见 Wave 362，gateway/vnet 为 live 门控）；补这些 harness 样本需要 TS+Go 双后端同时运行，属下一步可在有环境时推进的离线-不可达项。`gofmt`/build/userprefs 测试零失败。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 437（真实 rootless Podman 容器 preflight live 验证 — 解锁环境门控 gap）用户提供了 live 环境（podman 5.4.2 rootless + pasta 后端 + bun 1.3.13 + podman-compose 可用），开始攻此前被环境门控的容器 parity gap。本轮针对 `chapters.containers-runtime` 的 "real rootless Podman/cgroup/proxy-cache behavior remains unverified beyond fake-Podman fixtures" 待办，为 `GET /container-setup`（`containerSetup` handler）补**真实 podman**集成测试（`chapters/container_preflight_live_test.go`，此前所有容器测试都用 `prependFakePodman` shell 假脚本，从未跑过真实 podman）。`podmanRootlessAvailable` helper 保守探测（podman 存在 + `--version` 非空 + `podman info` 可取 `RootlessNetworkCmd`），任一探测失败则 `t.Skip`（保证无 podman 的 CI 仍绿）；本机有真实 podman 时实际运行，断言：(1) 响应 payload 形状与 TS `getContainerSetupStatus` 的 `ContainerSetupStatus` 契约一致（podman/podmanCompose/composeProvider/passt/rootlessNetwork 均为带 `ok` 的对象 + `allReady` 布尔）；(2) 真实 podman 存在 → `podman.ok=true` 且 version 非空（验证 `commandVersion` 真实路径，fake 脚本无法验证）；(3) pasta/passt 主机 → `rootlessNetwork.ok=true` 且 backend 字符串经 `mustCommandOutput`+`podman info` 真实往返（本机实测 backend=pasta，验证 `isPastaBackend` 逻辑同源的真实探测路径）。测试在本机真实 podman 下 PASS（2.0s），全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`gofmt`/build/`git diff --check` 干净。本轮把容器 preflight 从"仅 fake-Podman 覆盖"升级为"真实 rootless podman live 验证"，缩小 `chapters.containers-runtime` 的 fixture-only 缺口；后续可在 live 环境继续攻容器生命周期（start/stop/pause/port 分配/proxy）的真实 podman 验证与 TS-Go 双后端 harness 采样。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 438（真实 podman 容器 inspect 解析 live 验证）延续 Wave 437 的 live 容器攻坚，针对 `chapters.containers-runtime` 的 fixture-only 缺口，为 `inspectContainerExposedPorts`（解析真实 `podman inspect --format {{json .Config.ExposedPorts}}` 输出）补真实容器集成测试（`chapters/container_inspect_live_test.go`）。手动确认真实 podman 5.4.2 对 `--expose 8080 --expose 9090` 的容器输出 `{"8080/tcp":{},"9090/tcp":{}}`，正是 Go parser 期望的格式。测试 `realPodmanForInspect` 探测（podman 存在 + version 非空），无则 `t.Skip`；有则真实 `podman run -d --expose ... hello-world`（已预拉取的微镜像，立即退出，仅需容器记录供 inspect），`t.Cleanup` 中 `podman rm -f`。断言：(1) `inspectContainerExposedPorts` 对真实容器返回排序后的 `[8080,9090]`（验证 parser 对真实 podman JSON 格式的兼容，fake fixture 无法保证格式不漂移）；(2) 不存在的容器 id → nil（inspect 失败路径无 panic）；(3) 空白 id → nil（短路）。本机真实 podman 下 PASS（0.9s），全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`gofmt`/build/`git diff --check` 干净。registry 可达（实测可 `podman pull`），为后续真实容器生命周期（compose up/start/pause/port-allocate）live 验证铺路。本轮把容器 inspect 端口解析从"仅 fake-Podman 覆盖"升级为"真实 podman JSON 格式 live 验证"。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 439（真实 podman 容器 IP 解析 live 验证 + pasta 空 IP 行为确认）延续 live 容器攻坚，为 `inspectContainerIP`（解析真实 `podman inspect --format {{range .NetworkSettings.Networks}}{{.IPAddress}} {{end}}`）补真实容器集成测试（`chapters/container_ip_live_test.go`）。**确认一处真实网络行为**：rootless **pasta** 后端下运行中容器的 `NetworkSettings.Networks` IP 为**空**（实测 `--network` 默认 pasta 时 IP_RAW=[]），这是 NarraFork 生产默认（rootless pasta）的真实场景，也正是 proxy mode 需用 pasta/passt 而非容器 IP 解析的原因；要拿到确定的非空 IP 必须显式 `--network bridge`（实测得 `10.88.0.13`）。测试用 `--network bridge --entrypoint sleep golang:alpine 60` 起长驻容器（保证 network namespace 已就绪），断言：(1) `inspectContainerIP` 提取的首个 IP 是合法 `net.ParseIP`（验证 `strings.Fields(out)[0]` 对真实 podman 多网络输出的解析）；(2) 不存在的容器 id → ""（inspect 失败路径无 panic）；(3) 空白 id → ""（短路）；环境若返回空 IP 则 skip IP 形状断言（保守，不在无 bridge IP 的环境误失败）。本机真实 podman + bridge 下 PASS（11s，含镜像层准备），`t.Cleanup` 中 `podman rm -f` 清理无残留。全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`gofmt`/build/`git diff --check` 干净。至此容器 inspect 的两条真实解析路径（exposed ports、network IP）均完成 live 验证；并记录了 pasta 空-IP 这一影响 proxy 设计的真实网络事实。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 440（真实 podman compose 生命周期 + ps JSON 解析 live 验证）延续 live 容器攻坚，针对 `chapters.containers-runtime` 的 compose 实例列举解析路径，跑**真实 `podman compose up -d` → `ps --format json` → `down`** 完整生命周期并验证 `parseContainerJSONList` + `normalizeContainerInstance` 对真实 podman-compose 输出的解析（`chapters/compose_ps_live_test.go`）。手动确认真实 podman 5.4.2 + podman-compose 1.5.0 的 `compose ps --format json` 输出为对象数组，含 `Id`（完整容器 ID）、`Image`、`Names`（数组如 `["x_app_1"]`）、`State`（"running"）、`Labels` 等字段（无顶层 `Service` 字段，serviceName 经 label/名称回退派生）。测试：`composeVersion` 解析真实 provider（本机 `podman compose`），在**仓库内**临时子目录（`os.MkdirTemp(".", ...)`，绝不写 /tmp）写最小 compose.yml（golang:alpine + sleep 120），经 `composeCommandForFile` 构建命令真实 `up -d`，`t.Cleanup` 中 `down` + `RemoveAll`；断言解析出的实例 containerId 非空、chapterId 正确加戳、serviceName 非空、image 含 "golang"、status 属归一化状态集（running/created/paused/stopped/removed）。本机真实 podman 下 PASS（13.8s，含 up/ps/down 全生命周期），无残留临时目录/容器；全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`gofmt`/build/`git diff --check` 干净。至此容器运行时的三条真实 podman 解析路径（preflight W437、exposed ports W438、network IP W439、compose ps W440）均完成 live 验证，`chapters.containers-runtime` 的 fixture-only 缺口已大幅收敛（仅余完整 start/stop 编排与 TS-Go 双后端 harness 采样待后续）。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 441（真实 podman compose pause/unpause 状态机 live 验证）延续 live 容器攻坚，针对 dormant/wake（pause/unpause）容器状态机此前仅 fake-Podman 覆盖的缺口，跑**真实 `compose up → pause → unpause → down`** 周期并验证 `normalizeContainerStatus` 对真实 podman State 值的映射（`chapters/compose_pause_live_test.go`）。手动确认真实 podman 5.4.2 + podman-compose 1.5.0 的 `compose pause`/`unpause` 命令可用且状态正确转换：up 后 State=running → pause 后 State=paused → unpause 后 State=running。测试经 `composePsState`（复用 `parseContainerJSONList`+`normalizeContainerInstance` 解析真实 `ps --format json`）断言三段转换下归一化状态依次为 running→paused→running，证明 `normalizeContainerStatus` 的 "paused"→paused、"running"→running 映射对真实 podman 输出成立（fake fixture 无法保证）。仓库内临时子目录（`os.MkdirTemp(".", ...)`，绝不写 /tmp）+ `t.Cleanup` down/RemoveAll。本机真实 podman 下 PASS（15.3s，含 up/pause/unpause/down 全周期），无残留；全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`gofmt`/build/`git diff --check` 干净。至此容器运行时的 dormant/wake 状态机（pause/unpause）也由真实 podman 背书，加上 W437-440 的 preflight/exposed-ports/network-IP/compose-ps，`chapters.containers-runtime` 的核心真实 podman 解析与状态转换路径已系统 live 验证完毕（仅余 host-port 分配/proxy-cache 的完整 start 编排端到端与 TS-Go 双后端 harness 采样待后续）。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 442（完整 start 编排 live 验证尝试 → 发现 compose provider 选择的环境依赖性，移除不稳定测试并诚实记录）尝试为非 proxy host-port 模式的完整 `POST /containers/start` 编排（allocate port → compose up → 列举 → 持久化）补真实 podman 端到端测试。**调试发现一处真实环境依赖性（非 Go bug）**：经 `startChapterContainersLocked` → `runLimitedEnvTimeout`（`cmd.Env = os.Environ()+extra`）运行 `podman compose up -d` 时，podman 选中的外部 compose provider 是 `/usr/libexec/docker/cli-plugins/docker-compose`（docker-compose），它尝试连 Docker daemon socket（未运行）→失败；而直接 `podman-compose`（`~/.local/bin`）可用。即 `podman compose` 子命令的 provider 选择依赖环境（PATH/DOCKER_HOST/插件存在性），可能选中需要 docker daemon 的 docker-compose 插件——这对 Go 与 TS **同样成立**（两端 `composeVersion`/`getContainerSetupStatus` 都先探测 `podman compose`，其 `version` 成功不保证 `up` 能连后端），故**非 Go-vs-TS parity 差异**，而是宿主 compose provider 配置问题。鉴于该 start 编排测试依赖非确定的 provider 选择会 flaky，且 start 编排逻辑本身已由 fake-podman 测试（`TestContainerStartProxyModeRecordsProxyMetadataWithFakePodman` 等）覆盖，**移除了该不稳定的 live 测试**（`container_start_live_test.go`，已 `git diff` 确认净零删除、未误删他人改动），不留 flaky 测试污染套件。W440/W441 的 compose ps/pause live 测试因走直接 `exec.CommandContext` 继承测试进程纯净 env、podman 在该 env 下选中可用 provider 而稳定通过，保留不动。全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`gofmt`/build/`git diff --check` 干净，无 stray 容器/临时目录。**诚实结论**：完整 start 编排的 live 验证受宿主 compose-provider 配置（docker-compose 插件 vs podman-compose）非确定性阻塞，需先统一/固定 provider（如 unset docker-compose 插件或让 podman 优先 podman-compose）才能稳定 live 验证；这是真实但属环境配置层面的待办，非 Go 代码 parity 缺口。容器运行时核心解析/状态路径（preflight/exposed-ports/network-IP/compose-ps/pause-unpause）已由 W437-441 真实 podman 背书。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 443（start 编排 live 验证二次深挖：provider 可固定，但 up-vs-ps env/项目名 + stale-state 致测试不可靠，再次诚实移除）延续 W442，先解决其 provider 阻塞：实测 `PODMAN_COMPOSE_PROVIDER=$(which podman-compose)` 可强制 `podman compose` 用可用的 podman-compose（绕开需 docker daemon 的 docker-compose 插件），且 `t.Setenv` 经 `os.Environ()` 传入运行时生效。又修正第二个测试陷阱：`loadTempSettings` 会 `t.Setenv("HOME", tmpdir)`，把 podman 存储重定向到空临时 HOME → compose up 失败/超时且 cleanup 报 permission denied；改用 `Service{DB: database}`（Settings=nil 即用默认端口范围 10000-20000）保留真实 HOME 后该问题消失。但同步调试（`startChapterContainersLocked` 直接调用）暴露**第三层问题**：`compose up` 真实跑了 13.6s 且返回 nil，但 `liveComposeContainerInstances` 的 `compose ps --format json` 返回 **0 items**（持久化 0 行），而手动用相同 `COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME` env 跑 ps 却能看到容器——指向 up 与 ps 之间的 env/项目名传递或 stale 跨运行项目名冲突，且与 provider 选择、`runLimitedEnv` 的 env 合并纠缠，行为非确定。鉴于该 live 测试受多层环境非确定性（provider 选择、HOME/存储、项目名碰撞、stale 容器）影响而 flaky，且 start 编排逻辑已由 fake-podman 测试覆盖，**再次移除不稳定的 live 测试与调试文件**（`container_start_live_test.go`/`zz_debug*_test.go`，`git status` 确认无残留），并清理了 stale 的 `narrafork_*`/`*_web_1` 探测容器。保留的 W437-441 live 测试全绿、全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、build/`git diff --check` 干净。**诚实结论（升级 W442）**：完整 start 编排的 live 验证不仅受 compose-provider 选择影响，还涉及"up 用的 env/项目名 ↔ ps 列举用的 env/项目名"一致性这一可能的真实运行时细节——但要在本共享环境隔离验证它，需先消除 provider 非确定性 + 每测试唯一项目名 + 干净存储隔离三者，工程成本高且易 flaky；当前判断为**环境/测试基建层面的待办**，已记录待后续在受控环境（或固定 provider + 唯一 namespace）下重做，而非贸然 ship 不稳定测试。下一步 live 方向更适合转向 bun 双后端 harness 的跨后端响应采样（确定性更高）。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 444（bun 双后端 harness live 跑通 + usage-history/reviews/terminals 跨后端采样补齐）转向确定性更高的 live 方向：先验证 `go_backend/testtools/ts-go-api-contract-diff.ts` 双后端 harness 在本 live 环境**实际可跑通**（`bun` 1.3.13 + node_modules 已就绪：harness 自建 Go 二进制、spawn TS、注册用户、跨后端结构化 diff，实测 `bun ...` 退出 0、`diffs:[]`、WS 开闭正常）。随后补齐 Wave 436 标记的三个**从未被 harness 采样**的路由组（对照 Go 注册前缀与 harness 采样集发现 `/api/usage-history`、`/api/reviews`、`/api/terminals` 零采样）：在 `collect()` 新增 11 个跨后端样本——usage-history（providers/stats/timeseries/list 的 200、user token 的 403 forbidden）、reviews（`/{id}/conclusion` 与 `/by-source/{id}/conclusion` 缺失场景 200）、terminals（缺 scope 的 400、空 list/tabs 的 200、缺失 detail 的 404）。harness 实跑确认 11 个样本全部 **TS=Go 状态码与结构一致**（usageHistory* 200、forbidden 403、reviewConclusion* 200、terminalsMissingScope 400、terminalsListEmpty/TabsEmpty 200、terminalDetailMissing 404），顶层 `diffs:[]` 证明 TS 与 Go 在这三组路由的响应 status/keys/shape 全部对齐。纯 harness（测试工具）增量，无运行时改动；`bunx @biomejs/biome check` 该文件通过；Go 全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`gofmt`/build/`git diff --check` 干净。此前这三组路由的 TS↔Go 响应 parity 仅由各自单后端 focused test 保证，现升级为跨后端契约锁定，补上了 Wave 436 记录的 harness 覆盖空白（确定性方向，规避了 W442/443 容器 start 的多层环境非确定性）。剩余未采样前缀 `/api/project-db`、`/api/gateway`、`/api/vnet`、`/api/admin` 中，gateway/vnet 为 live 门控、exploration-groups 系 Go-only（W362），project-db/admin 可后续同模式补样。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 445（admin/users 跨后端采样补齐 + project-db/diagnostics 不采样的诚实判定）延续 W444 双后端 harness 采样，先逐一核对 W444 标记的剩余前缀避免 false diff：(1) **`/api/project-db` 不采样**——Go 的 `/api/project-db/status`、`/api/project-db/` 是 Go-specific stub（其 status 消息本身就写"Use /api/projects/:id/backup/..."），TS 把 project-db 路由挂在 `/api/projects/:id/backup/*` 而非 `/api/project-db/*`，采样会造成 TS-404-vs-Go-200 的假 diff（同 exploration-groups 的 Go-only 情形）；(2) **`/api/admin/diagnostics` 不采样**——Go 是极简 stub `{goRoutines, memory:{}, eventLoopLagMs}`，TS 是富 shape（timestamp/uptime/cpu/详细 memory/narrator counts/watchers/terminals），属已知 shape 简化分歧，采样会真实 diff（非本轮要锁定的对齐项）；(3) **`/api/admin/terminals`** 同理含 processes/进程映射差异，暂不采。**实际采样**：`/api/admin/users`——逐字段核对 Go(`listUsers`)与 TS 均返回 `{id,username,role,avatarColor,avatarImageId,createdAt}` 数组，形状完全一致，遂新增两个样本：`adminUsersList`（admin token → 200 用户列表）、`adminUsersForbiddenForUser`（regular token → 403）。harness 实跑确认 TS=Go（200/200、403/403），顶层 `diffs:[]`。纯 harness 增量、无运行时改动；`bunx @biomejs/biome check` 通过、Go 全套件 `GOPROXY=off go test ./...` 零失败、`gofmt`/build/`git diff --check` 干净。本轮把 `/api/admin` 的 users 列表与权限门控升级为跨后端契约锁定；诚实记录 project-db（Go-only stub）与 admin diagnostics/terminals（已知 shape 简化）不纳入 harness 的理由，避免制造假 diff。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 362（Changelog harness sample + exploration-groups TS-side asymmetry finding）继续把本批次新加契约的公开路由升级为跨后端契约锁定。(1) 为 `GET /api/changelog` 新增 TS-Go harness 样本——此前 Wave 357/358 补了 health 契约与 embedded fallback 实现，但 harness 从未跨后端比对 changelog 响应。新增样本后 harness 确认 TS 与 Go 均返回 `status:200`、`kind:array`、`shape:array`，`diffs:[]`（两端 `contentType` 略有差异 `application/json` vs `application/json; charset=utf-8`，harness 只比对 status/keys/shape 不比对 header，属良性差异）。(2) **审计发现并诚实记录一处 TS-side 不对称**：`/api/exploration-groups`（list/get/create/update/decide/abandon）在 Go 完整实现且被前端（`frontend/lib/api/chapters.ts:204-228`）调用，但 **TypeScript 后端完全没有该路由**（`server/` 中 `explorationGroups` 仅作为 graph/projects 内的 DB 表引用，无任何 `/api/exploration-groups` 路由 handler/service）。这意味着在 TS 后端下这些前端调用会 404，而 Go 后端正确实现——属于 **Go 超出 TS parity 的方向**（符合 cutover 目标）。因此**有意不把 exploration-groups 加入 TS-Go harness**（会产生 TS 404 vs Go 200 的虚假 diff）；此处记录该不对称供未来 TS-side 修复或正式接受为 Go-only 增强。

- 2026-06-12 — Wave 358（Changelog embedded fallback — functional parity fix）修复 Wave 357 审计中发现的真实功能分歧：Go changelog handler 此前只读文件系统候选目录，编译二进制无 sidecar `changelogs/` 时返回空数组，而 TypeScript 构建会把 changelog JSON 嵌入二进制（`embedded-changelog.ts`）。本轮新增 `scripts/generate-go-changelog.ts` 生成器（镜像 `scripts/generate-go-drizzle-migrations.ts` 模式），扫描 `changelogs/*.json` 并生成 `go_backend/internal/app/changelog_embedded.go`（69 条按 semver 降序的 embedded 条目）；`changelogHandler` 在文件系统候选目录全部为空时回退到 `embeddedChangelogEntries`。新增 focused test `TestChangelogFallsBackToEmbeddedEntriesWhenNoSidecarDirectory`（env 覆盖指向空目录时仍返回完整 embedded 条目且 semver 降序）。Health 把 `changelog.embeddedFallbackInBinary` 翻为 `true`、`filesystemOnly` 改为 `filesystemFirst:true` 并更新 reason。这是本批次首个从"补契约"升级为"补实现"的 wave——审计发现的分歧用代码修复而非仅记录。保持 `fullTSChangelogParity:false`（构建管线对 Go 二进制的 changelog 嵌入仍需在发布流程中按需重新生成）。

- 2026-06-12 — Wave 327（Plan-reflection WS 事件族接受性差异契约）把 `plan_reflection_started` / `plan_reflection_resolved` / `plan_reflection_stopped` 的剩余 WS gap 收口为明确的运行时模型差异。审计确认这些 TypeScript 事件来自 `server/lib/agent/tools/exit-plan-reflection.ts` 的异步 `planReflectionAutoApprove` loop：`running` 广播 started，`awaiting_user` 广播 stopped，最终状态广播 resolved。Go 的 ExitPlanMode 设计已在 Wave 315 明确收窄为始终经过显式用户审批；它不会运行 TS 的跳过用户审批自动反思 loop，也就没有独立的 plan-reflection 生命周期可广播。用户可见契约由 `permission_request` / `permission_resolved` 表达，`compactAfter` 的用户审批后上下文重置仍然支持。Health 的 `narrator.planMode.exitPlanApproval` 现在显式暴露 `planReflectionWSEvents:false`、`tsPlanReflectionEvents:[plan_reflection_started, plan_reflection_resolved, plan_reflection_stopped]`、`goApprovalEventFamily:[permission_request, permission_resolved]`、`stopPlanReflectionRoute`、`stopPlanReflectionMode:"permission-cancel-fallback-without-plan-reflection-loop"` 和 `planReflectionWSReason`；`route_contract_test` 与 phase3 matrix 同步。无生产行为变更；这是 capability contract/accepted divergence 收口。HTTP 响应形状未变，contract harness 不受影响（沿用本轮 0 diffs）。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 326（Danger-reflection stop-route 同步模型契约）继续收紧 Wave 325 的反思事件族边界。审计确认 TypeScript 的 `POST /api/narrators/permissions/:requestId/stop-reflection` 在停止异步 danger-reflection loop 后广播 `danger_reflection_stopped`，语义是“模型反思暂停，回到用户手动审批”；Go 的 danger reflection 是同步内联模型，没有可被用户接管的长驻反思 loop，因此 stop-route 在可处理的持久反思状态上以 `danger_reflection_resolved(decision=aborted)` 收束反思指示器。前端 `useNarratorWS.ts` 已显式接受 `danger_reflection_resolved` 的 `decision:"aborted"`，所以这是前端兼容的同步模型差异，而不是漏发必须实现的事件。Health 的 `narrator.dangerReflectionClassification` 现在显式暴露 `resolvedDecisions:[allow, deny, error, aborted]`、`stopReflectionRoute`、`stopReflectionEmits:"danger_reflection_resolved(decision=aborted)"`、`stopReflectionTSEmits:"danger_reflection_stopped"` 和 `stopReflectionParity` 说明；`route_contract_test` 与 phase0 matrix 同步断言这些字段。无生产行为变更；这是 capability contract/accepted divergence 收口。HTTP 响应形状未变，contract harness 不受影响（0 diffs）。
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 325 (Danger-reflection `started`/`resolved` WS broadcasts) continues the WS event-family audit. TypeScript emits `danger_reflection_started` when a dangerous tool call enters reflection and `danger_reflection_resolved`/`danger_reflection_stopped` on outcome (`server/services/narrator-permission.ts`), driving the frontend "reflecting" indicator (`frontend/hooks/useNarratorWS.ts:457-473`). Go ran danger reflection synchronously inline (`runDangerReflection` before tool execution) but emitted no `danger_reflection_started` and only emitted `danger_reflection_resolved` from the interactive HTTP permission handler — so during an auto-reflection run the frontend never saw the reflecting state. Go now broadcasts `danger_reflection_started` (`{narratorId, toolUseId, toolName}`) before running the reflection and `danger_reflection_resolved` (`{narratorId, toolUseId, decision, reason}`) after, with `decision` ∈ `allow`/`deny`/`error`. A focused test (`TestRunBasicAgentDangerReflectionBroadcastsStartedAndResolved`) drives a Bash call through danger reflection with a capturing hub and asserts both events fire with the correct toolUseId and a `deny` decision. Health's `narrator.dangerReflectionClassification` gains `wsEvents:[danger_reflection_started, danger_reflection_resolved]`, `emitsStartedEvent:true`, `emitsResolvedEvent:true`, `interactiveStopEvent:false` (Go reflection is synchronous with no user-takeover pause, so `danger_reflection_stopped` is intentionally not emitted; the reflecting indicator is driven by the started/resolved pair), and `resolvedDecisions:[allow, deny, error]`. `route_contract_test` assertions updated. Additive WS broadcasts, no HTTP route change, so the harness is unaffected (0 diffs). `danger_reflection_stopped` remains a documented accepted divergence tied to Go's synchronous (non-interactive) danger-reflection model.
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 324 (WS event-family claim-list accuracy correction) — no production behavior change. Continuing the WS audit, verified that several event families listed in `narrator.wsEvents.unclaimedContract.unclaimedFamilies` are in fact already emitted by Go and were merely mislabeled: `message_updated`/`messages_deleted` (`rollback_edit.go` broadcast on edit/rollback), `browser_session_count` (`browser_sessions.go:227`), `browser_session_lifecycle` (`browser_sessions.go:99` emits `browser_session_<created|updated|closed|artifact_saved>`), and `container_status_changed` (`chapters/handlers.go:1799` broadcasts `{narratorId, chapterId, containerStatus, action, status, ok}` to every chapter narrator, a superset of the TS `{narratorId, chapterId, containerStatus}` shape consumed by `frontend/hooks/useNarratorWS.ts`). These five families are moved into a new `claimedAdditionalFamilies` list; `unclaimedFamilies` now contains only the genuinely-unemitted families (`reflection` — Go emits question/goal/danger reflection resolved but not `danger_reflection_started`/`plan_reflection_*` which tie to the narrowed auto-approve path; `compact_progress`; `foreground_tool_progress`; `long_running_tool_output`; `active_run_reconnect`). `route_contract_test` assertions updated. No new broadcasts were added; this corrects the health metadata so it no longer understates Go's WS coverage. The genuinely-unclaimed families remain tracked under their respective `fullTSWSEventParity:false` / tool-progress / compact-recovery boundaries.
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 323 (Foreground subagent completion `subagent_status_changed` broadcast) continues the WS event-family audit. The TypeScript subagent runtime (`server/services/subagent-executor.ts:206`, `subagent-detach.ts:171`) broadcasts `subagent_status_changed` to the **parent** narrator when a foreground subagent finishes, so the parent UI updates the subagent's status badge (`frontend/hooks/useNarratorWS.ts` / `useNarratorPanelWS.ts` consume it). Go already emitted `subagent_started`/`subagent_attached`/`subagent_detached` but never `subagent_status_changed` on completion, leaving the parent's subagent badge stale until refresh. Go now broadcasts `subagent_status_changed` (`{narratorId:parentId, subagentNarratorId, status:"idle", substatus}`) from all foreground-subagent completion paths via a new `broadcastSubagentStatusChanged` helper called in `finishAgentRunSuccess` (substatus `["manual_override"]`/`["unread"]`), `finishAgentRunError` (`["error"]`), `finishAgentRunTimeout` (`["timeout"]`), `finishAgentRunSilentDisconnect` (`["silent_disconnect"]`), and `finishAgentRunCancelled` (`[]`); the helper is a no-op for non-subagent narrators, orphan subagents, and background subagents (which already drive the parent via `background_task_*` events). A focused test (`TestForegroundSubagentCompletionBroadcastsSubagentStatusChangedToParent`) runs a foreground subagent to completion and asserts the parent receives `subagent_status_changed` with the subagent id + idle status while the subagent itself still gets its own `status` broadcast; the existing background-subagent test confirms background completions do NOT emit the foreground event. Health updates: `narrator.wsEvents.unclaimedContract` moves `subagent_started`/`subagent_status_changed` out of `unclaimedFamilies` into a new `claimedSubagentLifecycleEvents` list, and `narrator.subagents` gains `lifecycleWSEvents`/`statusChangedOnCompletion:true`/`statusChangedOutcomes`. `route_contract_test` assertions updated. Additive WS broadcast, no HTTP route change, so the harness is unaffected (0 diffs). `fullTSSubagentRuntimeParity:false` remains because `subagent_suspended`/`subagent_todos_updated`/`subagent_warning` and parent-promise reblocking still require the deeper TS subagent runtime.
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 322 (Model/reasoning-effort settings-mutation WS broadcasts) closes a WebSocket event-family parity gap found by auditing the frontend-listened event types against Go's emitters. The TypeScript `PATCH /api/narrators/:id/model` and `PATCH /api/narrators/:id/reasoning-effort` routes broadcast `model_changed` and `model_settings_changed` (via `updateNarratorModel`/`updateNarratorReasoningEffort`) so connected clients update the displayed model and the live runtime-settings indicator (`frontend/hooks/useNarratorWS.ts` consumes `model_changed` → `onModelChanged`). Go's `updateModel` and `updateReasoningEffort` handlers persisted the field but emitted no WS event, leaving other connected clients stale until a manual refresh — unlike the adjacent `permission_mode_changed`/`relaxed_plan_changed`/`title_updated`/`reflection_overrides_changed` broadcasts which Go already mirrored. Go now broadcasts `model_changed` (`{narratorId, model}`) plus `model_settings_changed` (`{narratorId, model, reasoningEffort, status:"updated", applyAt:"next_request"}`) from the model PATCH, and `model_settings_changed` from the reasoning-effort PATCH carrying the persisted model + new effort. A focused test extends the shared `TestNarratorSettingsMutationsBroadcastTSCompatibleEvents` with the `model_changed` case and adds `TestReasoningEffortAndModelBroadcastModelSettingsChanged` covering the reasoning-effort `model_settings_changed` event and the model-change dual broadcast carrying the persisted reasoning effort. Health's `narrator.wsEvents` gains a `settingsMutation` block enumerating the settings-mutation event family (`title_updated`, `permission_mode_changed`, `relaxed_plan_changed`, `reflection_overrides_changed`, `model_changed`, `model_settings_changed`, `custom_traits_changed`, `draft_changed`) with `modelChangeBroadcast`/`modelSettingsBroadcast`/`reasoningEffortBroadcast` flags and the `status`/`applyAt` contract; it also records `pruneBoundaryEventEmitted:false` with a reason pointing at the tracked `narrator.compact.replacementContract.exactPruneBoundaryParity:false` gap, since the TS `prune_boundary` event comes from the not-yet-ported dynamic-pruning-window runtime rather than a settings PATCH. These are additive WS broadcasts that do not change any HTTP route response, so the cross-backend harness is unaffected (0 diffs). Note: the TS broadcasts are gated on an in-memory `active?.alive` runtime session; Go emits unconditionally on the persisted PATCH so multi-client model-display sync works without requiring an in-memory active session — an accepted behavioral widening that is strictly more useful for client sync, not a divergence in event shape.

- 2026-06-12 — Wave 320 (Assistant message text edit without regeneration) implements the actual narrator message-edit feature behind migration 0042 (Wave 319 synced only the schema metadata). TypeScript exposes `POST /api/narrators/:id/edit-message/:messageId` (`editAssistantMessage`) which persists edited assistant text for display and subsequent history assembly **without** truncating following messages or re-running the agent; the edit metadata (`editedAt`/`editedBy`/`originalContentJson`) lives on the message row, never inside `contentJson`, so it is never sent to the AI provider, and the frontend `MessageBubble` reads these fields to show an "edited" indicator and reveal the original text. Go previously had only `edit-and-regenerate` (user-message edit + rerun) and did not surface the edit fields in message responses. This wave adds: (1) the `Message` struct + `MarshalJSON` now carry `editedAt`/`editedBy`/`originalContentJson` (always present, null when unset, matching TS Drizzle row serialization); `scanMessage` and the four shared 16-column message SELECTs (`loadMessagesByRefs`, children, single-by-id, `messageForNarratorRef`) read the new columns; (2) `copyOnWriteOverrides` gains edit-metadata fields and both the non-shared UPDATE and the copy-on-write INSERT/`copyMessageForNarratorTx` paths write/preserve `edited_at`/`edited_by`/`original_content_json` (re-edits keep the first-edit original); (3) a new `Service.EditAssistantMessage` mirrors the TS service — validates assistant-role (404 otherwise), builds the edited content-json via `buildEditedAssistantContentJSON` (replace first text block, drop extra text blocks, preserve non-text blocks), sets `editedAt`/`editedBy`, preserves `originalContentJson` only on the first edit, copy-on-write protects shared/forked messages, and broadcasts `message_updated` (+ `full_reload` when the message id changes); (4) the `POST /{id}/edit-message/{messageId}` route + handler enforce the `editAssistantMessageSchema` Zod bound (content 1..100000), reject editing on a running subagent (400 "Cannot edit on a running subagent"), and reset an archived narrator to idle, mirroring the TS route guards. Focused Go tests cover the first-edit text replacement + non-text-block preservation + `originalContentJson` capture, the re-edit original-content preservation, and the non-assistant/missing-message rejections. The test `narrator_messages` schemas in `agent_event_integration_test.go` and `critical_path_e2e_test.go` gained the three columns so the 16-column scans resolve. Health adds `narrator.rollbackEditRegenerate.editAssistantMessage` (route, `persistsEditedText:true`, `rerunsAgent:false`, `assistantRoleOnly:true`, `contentBounds:"1..100000"`, `editedMetadataOffContentJSON:true`, `originalContentPreservedFirstEdit:true`, copy-on-write/full-reload, running-subagent/archived guards, `messageReadFields:[editedAt,editedBy,originalContentJson]`) plus an `editMessage` route flag, with matching `route_contract_test` assertions. The cross-backend harness returns 0 diffs (TS already serializes these fields as null on unedited messages, so the additive Go fields preserve message-shape parity); like the existing rollback/edit-and-regenerate routes, the edit-message mutation is covered by focused Go tests rather than a harness fixture because it needs a seeded assistant-message tree.

This wave re-syncs the Go-side TS-owned-schema metadata: (1) regenerated `drizzle_migrations_embedded.go` via `scripts/generate-go-drizzle-migrations.ts` (now 43 migrations including 0042); (2) added the `0042_shocking_naoko` entry to `embeddedDrizzleMigrationSnapshot` in `drizzle_journal_snapshot.go` (hash `a905aa05…`, when `1781278512923`); (3) appended `edited_at`, `edited_by`, `original_content_json` to the `narrator_messages` entry in `canonicalMainSchema`; (4) regenerated the `bun-final` compat artifacts — `canonical_schema_contract.json` (now `expectedMigrationCount:43`, `expectedLastMigration:0042_shocking_naoko`), `drizzle_journal_snapshot.json`, and a freshly-captured `sqlite_schema_snapshot.json`/`.sha256` taken from a complete migrated DB (migrations + FTS5 setup + legacy optional `custom_subagents`/`mcp_servers` tables to mirror the curated baseline). The full `internal/db` suite, app, and narrators packages pass, and the cross-backend harness returns 0 diffs. Note: this wave only re-syncs the read-only schema-verification metadata; the actual narrator message-edit feature behavior (writing `edited_at`/`original_content_json` on edit) is not yet implemented in the Go agent runtime and remains a separate tracked behavior gap. **（Wave 360 更正：该 behavior gap 现已关闭——Go `Service.EditAssistantMessage`（`rollback_edit.go`，`POST /api/narrators/:id/edit-message/:messageId`）在编辑时写入 `edited_at`/`edited_by` 并在首次编辑保存 `original_content_json`，Wave 359 又补齐了 `Service.RestoreAssistantMessage`（`POST /api/narrators/:id/restore-message/:messageId`）清除编辑元数据；`TestEditAssistantMessageUpdatesTextAndPreservesOriginalContent`/`TestRestoreAssistantMessageRevertsToOriginalAndClearsEditMetadata` 等 focused tests 覆盖。此处历史注记保留，但实现状态以本更正为准。）**
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 318 (Filesystem reveal Linux-unsupported phantom-gap resolution) corrects misleading health metadata that implied Go was missing a feature. The `POST /api/fs/reveal` route opens the system file manager; the TypeScript route (`server/routes/fs.ts:145-176`) explicitly supports only macOS (`open`) and Windows (`explorer`) and throws a 400 `ValidationError("Opening file manager is not supported on this platform")` on Linux — TypeScript itself does not implement Linux `xdg-open`. Go's reveal handler (`go_backend/internal/fs/handlers.go:160-197`) matches this exactly (darwin→open, windows→explorer, Linux→400 with the identical message), and the existing focused test `TestRevealUnsupportedPlatformMatchesTSValidation` already proves the Linux 400 + message parity. The prior health metadata (`revealLinuxXDGOpenParity:false` plus the `linux-xdg-open-parity-or-product-decision` requiredProof) framed this as an open gap or pending product decision, when in fact Go's Linux 400 is intentional TypeScript parity. This wave clarifies the contract: `fs.systemActionsContract` adds `revealLinuxUnsupportedMatchesTS:true` and an updated reason explaining neither backend opens a Linux file manager; `fs.replacementContract` adds `linuxRevealUnsupportedMatchesTSProof:true` and the `linux-reveal-unsupported-matches-ts-400` sampled proof, and removes the misleading `linux-xdg-open-parity-or-product-decision` from `requiredProofs` (resolved as an accepted no-op). `revealLinuxXDGOpenParity:false` is retained as a factual descriptor (neither backend implements xdg-open). No production behavior changed; this is a metadata-accuracy correction that removes a phantom required proof. The `route_contract_test.go` assertions were updated to match. The remaining fs replacement gates (windows/macOS reveal smoke, frontend file-picker browser smoke, large-file preview browser behavior, TS-Go filesystem action diff) stay open as genuine runtime smokes.

- 2026-06-12 — Wave 316 (Project-skill write routes) closes a real functional parity gap: Go's `RegisterSkills` only registered the project-skill **read** routes (`GET /`, `GET /{name}`, `GET /{name}/files/*`) while TypeScript (`server/routes/skills.ts`) also exposes the project-skill **write** routes `POST /api/skills`, `PUT /api/skills/{name}`, and `DELETE /api/skills/{name}` (all `?projectId=...`). Go now implements all three, mirroring the TypeScript `skillService.createProjectSkill`/`updateProjectSkill`/`deleteProjectSkill` behavior: the `createProjectSkillSchema`/`updateProjectSkillSchema` Zod bounds (name ≤100, description ≤2000, content ≤200000) reject out-of-range bodies with 400; create sanitizes the directory name with the TypeScript `sanitizeSkillDirName` algorithm (lowercase, `[^a-z0-9\u4e00-\u9fff_-]`→`-`, collapse hyphens, trim) and rejects an empty result as `Invalid skill name`; duplicate names and pre-existing directories return 400; create returns the parsed skill with 201; update resolves the existing project skill (404 when missing), defaults the name to the current name, rejects an empty description, rejects rename conflicts, and writes the new `SKILL.md`; delete removes the skill directory (404 when missing) and returns `{ok:true}`. Both write helpers enforce path safety mirroring the TypeScript guards: `assertSkillPathInsideProject` (lexical + realpath inside-project check) and `assertNoSkillSymlinkAncestor` (no existing path component may be a symlink). Without a project database the routes return the existing `project.skills` `FEATURE_DISABLED` fallback. Focused Go tests cover create-201/list-get visibility, duplicate-400, the three Zod-bound rejections, invalid-sanitized-name-400, update success/404, delete success/404 with directory removal, the no-DB fallback, and the `sanitizeProjectSkillDirName` parity cases. The TS-Go contract harness adds nine `projectSkillWrite*` samples (create/detail/duplicate/overlong-name/update-missing/update-valid/delete-missing/delete-valid/detail-after-delete) against the shared `p_search_fixture` project, all identical across backends (0 diffs). Health's `content.skills.routes` adds `projectCreate`/`projectUpdate`/`projectDelete`, the `projectSkillContract` gains `writeRoutesSupported:true` plus status/bound/path-safety fields, and `content.projectSkills.replacementContract` adds `projectSkillWriteRoutesProof:true` and the `project-skill-write-routes-create-update-delete-bounds` sampled proof; the replacement reason notes the read-only gap is closed while companion symlink/depth/frontend/golden/route-diff smokes remain the outstanding replacement gates.
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 315 (ExitPlanMode reflection auto-compact accepted-divergence contract) audits the TypeScript `cfbdbbb` commit's `ExitPlanMode auto-compact` change (`server/lib/agent/loop.ts`, `server/lib/agent/tools/exit-plan-reflection.ts`) and documents Go's behavior as an explicit accepted capability contract rather than a code gap. TypeScript adds a `confirm_compact` reflection decision and an `ExitPlanConfirmAndCompact` reflection tool, gated by the new `planReflectionAllowAutoCompact` config: when `planReflectionAutoApprove` is on (reflection skips user approval and executes the plan directly), the reflection gate may choose approval-with-context-reset, which adds the narrator to `pendingPlanCompact` and propagates a `compactAfter` flag through `plan_reflection` suggestions/events. This entire sub-feature lives inside TypeScript's `planReflectionAutoApprove` skip-user-approval path. Go intentionally narrows ExitPlanMode (documented 2026-05-27) to **always** route through an explicit user permission after readiness reflection — it prepares plan/reflection metadata, keeps `plan_mode=1`, and only exits on user approval — and does not implement the `planReflectionAutoApprove` skip path. Consequently the auto-compact reflection sub-feature does not apply: Go's reflection loop recognizes only `ExitPlanConfirm` (→ confirm) and `ExitPlanRevise` (→ revise), never `ExitPlanConfirmAndCompact`/`confirm_compact`. Crucially, the **user-driven** `compactAfter` context reset on plan approval is fully supported in Go (Waves of 2026-05-27: `continueMode:["continue","compact"]`, `continuePrompt`, `planCompact` honoring `compactAfter`), so the user-facing context-reset-on-approval capability has parity; only the reflection-auto-approve auto-compact branch is the accepted divergence. The existing `TestReflectionDecisionFromToolCall` was extended to assert `ExitPlanConfirmAndCompact` is rejected as an unsupported reflection action ("not supported"). Health's `planMode` capability is expanded with an `exitPlanApproval` block (`alwaysRequiresUserApproval:true`, `reflectionPreparesThenUserApproves:true`, `userCompactAfterSupported:true`, `continueModeMetadata:["continue","compact"]`, `editedPlanDiffPersisted:true`, `reflectionAutoApproveSkipsUser:false`, `reflectionAutoCompactSupported:false`, plus an `acceptedDivergence` rationale). No production code changed; this is a capability-contract documentation wave that makes the auto-approve/auto-compact narrowing explicit. With this, all Go-relevant changes from the `cfbdbbb` commit (skill multi-level context Waves 308-310, image-generation partial streaming Wave 312, bash danger severity Wave 313, aggregation disabled-provider skip Wave 314, and exit-plan auto-compact Wave 315) are reconciled.
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 314 (Aggregation routing skips disabled providers) closes a functional parity gap in model-aggregation resolution introduced by the TypeScript `aggregation routing skip disabled providers` change. TypeScript's `resolveAggregation` filters aggregation members through `isModelProviderDisabled`, where `getDisabledProviderPrefixes()` unions the explicit `agent.disabledProviders` list with the prefixes of any provider record (custom/openai/anthropic/nkp/nug/cline) carrying a per-provider `disabled` flag; it then routes by `routingMode` (priority = first enabled member, balanced = round-robin over enabled members). Go's `resolveAggregationFromRaw` previously returned the first non-empty member unconditionally, so it would resolve a disabled provider's model and ignored `routingMode`. Go now builds the same disabled-prefix set via a new `disabledProviderPrefixes(raw)` helper (both the `agent.disabledProviders` array and per-provider `disabled` flags across all six provider lists), filters members through `isModelProviderDisabled`, returns the first enabled member for priority mode, round-robins enabled members for balanced mode (process-local `aggRoundRobin` counter under a mutex, matching the TS in-memory counter semantics), and returns empty when all members are disabled (so callers fall through to the configured default model). Four focused tests cover: skip via `agent.disabledProviders`, skip via per-provider `disabled` flag, balanced round-robin over only enabled members, and empty resolution when every member is disabled. Health adds `narrator.agentRuntime.aggregationRouting` (`skipsDisabledProviders`, `honorsAgentDisabledProvidersList`, `honorsPerProviderDisabledFlag`, `disabledProviderSources`, `routingModes:["priority","balanced"]`, `balancedRoundRobin`, `emptyWhenAllMembersDisabled`, `stickyProviderPreference:false`). The `stickyProviderPreference` field documents an accepted narrowing: TS prefers a session sticky-provider member when present, but Go's `resolveAggregationFromRaw` is invoked from stateless before-turn resolution without the session sticky-provider context, so sticky preference is not implemented. This change affects only internal model resolution (not any HTTP route response), so the contract harness is unaffected.
- 2026-06-12 — Wave 313 (Bash danger-reflection severity-tier capability contract) audits the TypeScript `image generation streaming + rollback fix` commit's shell danger-severity change (`isUncertainShellDangerPattern`/`shellDangerPatternSeverity`: uncertain patterns → `medium`, definitely-dangerous → `high`) and documents Go's already-aligned behavior as an explicit accepted capability contract. TypeScript classifies bash danger via a full per-pattern `BashAnalysis` and now distinguishes uncertain execution patterns (path execution, unknown subcommand, not-in-allowlist) as `medium` severity from downloaded/nested/env-injected code as `high`. Go's `classifyBashDanger` reaches the same reflection-triggering severity tiers via a simplified token/allowlist policy heuristic: allowlist-miss/non-simple commands → `medium`, dangerous execution patterns (`curl … | bash`, command substitution, downloaded/nested code) and destructive deletes → `high`, git force-push → `critical`. The severity behavior is therefore at parity (it drives the same danger-reflection trigger threshold), while the internal danger summary wording differs (Go: "outside the safety allowlist" vs TS: "unclassified execution patterns") and is not a structured frontend contract field. The existing `TestDangerReflectionClassifiesRepresentativeToolCalls` test was extended with explicit dangerous-piped-execution and command-substitution `high`-severity cases alongside the existing `medium` non-simple-bash case to lock the high-vs-medium split. Health adds a `narrator.dangerReflectionClassification` capability (`severityTiers`, `levelEnum`, `bashUncertainSeverity:"medium"`, `bashDangerousSeverity:"high"`, `classificationStrategy:"go-simplified-token-policy"`, `severityBehaviorParity:true`, `summaryTextParity:false`, plus an `acceptedDecision` rationale). No production code changed; this is a capability-contract documentation wave that makes the divergent-architecture-but-aligned-severity behavior explicit rather than implicit.

## Replacement Readiness Summary

Status: **Unsafe / not replacement-ready**.

Readiness levels:

- **Unsafe to replace TS backend:** any P0 gap remains unresolved or unaccepted.
- **Compatibility beta:** all P0 gaps fixed or explicitly disabled with UI fallback; major P1 gaps documented.
- **Replacement candidate:** all P0 fixed, critical P1 fixed, smoke checklist passes, DB compatibility guard exists.

Current readiness judgment: **Unsafe to replace TS backend**. Every track now has verified/done evidence, and unresolved P0 blockers remain across API security/setup guards, DB/FTS/bootstrap, domain lifecycle semantics, narrator safety/runtime semantics, runtime/container/terminal/snapshot side effects, and frontend-visible contracts. Go may be a scoped compatibility backend only after the listed P0s are fixed or explicitly disabled with frontend-visible fallbacks and smoke coverage.

### Prioritized Execution Queue

1. **Close Goal 1 and Goal 4 first:** establish the durable matrix plus JSON/SSE/WS contract diff harness, because every later claim depends on it.
2. **Close Goals 2 and 3 before any replacement claim:** DB compatibility and TS rollback safety are the hard floor for all release decisions.
3. **Close Goals 5 through 9 for Agent-heavy beta:** MCP, Browser, subagent, rollback/edit-regenerate, and Codex parity determine whether real narrator workflows survive migration; rollback/edit-regenerate now has focused Go coverage but still needs broader TS-vs-Go fixture comparison before replacement readiness.
4. **Close Goals 10 through 13 for runtime parity:** chapter split, containers, VNet/terminal, update/gateway/benchmark remove the largest user-visible disabled/runtime gaps.
5. **Close Goal 14 last:** only after P0 gates are fixed or accepted as disabled-by-design should Go artifacts move from parallel beta to default release.

### Matrix Maintenance Rules

- Any new Go backend feature must update this matrix if it changes a row's readiness state, P0/P1/P2 classification, or acceptance gate.
- A row may move from `Blocked` to `Ready-gated` only with concrete tests or smoke evidence named in that row's track section.
- A P0 may be accepted without implementation only through the explicit disabled-by-design contract below.
- Default cutover remains forbidden while any matrix row is `Blocked` without an accepted fallback decision.

### Explicit Disable / Fallback Contract

A P0 feature may be accepted as `DISABLED-BY-DESIGN` only when all of the following are true:

1. **API refuses before mutation:** unsupported calls return a stable 4xx/501-style response before any DB, git, filesystem, process, container, or network side effect. Partial success is not allowed.
2. **Stable response shape:** the response includes machine-readable status such as `{ ok:false, code:"FEATURE_DISABLED", feature, reason }` or the existing TS-compatible error shape for that route. It must not look like a successful TS operation.
3. **Capability/readiness exposed:** `/api/health`, readiness/capability metadata, or the relevant resource payload exposes the disabled capability so clients can decide before invoking the action.
4. **Frontend-visible disabled state:** the UI hides or disables the action and explains the unsupported/degraded state; it must not offer a button that later reports misleading success.
5. **Smoke evidence:** smoke artifacts prove no 5xx, no misleading success, no unauthorized mutation, and no side effects for the disabled flow.

### TS Baseline / Compatibility Definition

`TS-compatible` means the same fixture is executed once against the TypeScript backend and once against the Go backend, then compared at the observable boundaries:

- HTTP status, content type, required response fields, and error shape/code.
- SSE/WS control and event names, required payload fields, reconnect behavior, and close/error semantics.
- DB rows created/updated/deleted for the relevant canonical tables.
- Git/worktree/filesystem/container/terminal side effects visible to users.
- Frontend observable state: rendered disabled/success/error state, toasts, uncaught exceptions, and route stability.

Extra fields are allowed if the frontend and existing API consumers ignore them; missing required TS fields, incompatible error codes, misleading success payloads, or extra mutations are not compatible.

## Track: API Contract Parity

Owner: spec-api
Status: Verified
Last update: 2026-05-14

### Scope

- Compare `server/app.ts`, `server/routes/*.ts` with `go_backend/internal/app/router.go` and
  `go_backend/internal/*/handlers.go`.
- Track missing routes, stub/degraded routes, auth/public boundary mismatches, and response shape
  mismatches. This section records API contract gaps only; deeper behavior belongs to domain,
  narrator, runtime, DB, and frontend tracks.

### Findings

- The Go router covers the main public routes and most TS route groups: health/auth/status,
  dependencies, shares, changelog, projects, chapters, graph/ruler/project backup, narrators,
  terminals, settings, fs, search, chapter edges, favorites, learning, routines, skills,
  workspaces, hooks, custom subagents, uploads, storage, notifications, reviews, MCP, admin,
  update, runtime, usage history, gateway, VNet, benchmarks, provider routes, and WebSockets.
  Evidence: `server/app.ts:95-153`, `server/app.ts:205-253`,
  `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:62-143`.
- Several Go routes are intentionally compatible-but-degraded rather than full TS equivalents.
  Examples include gateway/VNet/update self-apply, benchmark runner execution, Kiro chat SSE,
  storage cleanup, review fallbacks, and ruler compatibility routes with partial/degraded lifecycle
  semantics. Evidence: `go_backend/internal/systemroutes/handlers.go:501-584`,
  `go_backend/internal/systemroutes/handlers.go:648-790`,
  `go_backend/internal/systemroutes/handlers.go:1013-1024`,
  `go_backend/internal/misccompat/handlers.go:658-680`,
  `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:98-103`,
  `go_backend/internal/misccompat/handlers.go:2046-2054`.
- Go includes API routes that are not mounted by the current TS app (`/api/exploration-groups`,
  chapter batch-fork/split/cherry-pick/dependency-status/sync-upstream). Treat these as additive
  unless frontend starts relying on them under TS parity. Evidence:
  `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:115`, `go_backend/internal/chapters/handlers.go:41-46`;
  no matching mounts in `server/app.ts:205-253` or lines found in `server/routes/chapters.ts:44-493`.
- Go now implements the TS Git-unavailable request gate: `gitRequiredGuard` selectively returns
  `503` with `code: "GIT_NOT_INSTALLED"` for Git-dependent routes and avoids over-blocking setup,
  settings, provider, search, and standalone narrator routes. Evidence: `server/app.ts:155-203`,
  `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:209-233`, `go_backend/internal/app/route_contract_test.go:284`.
- Public/auth boundary mostly matches TS for health/auth/status/dependencies/changelog/shares and
  authenticated API groups. The older notification-sounds mutation mismatch is obsolete: TS mounts
  `/api/notification-sounds` after `requireAuth`, and current Go registers notification-sounds inside
  the authenticated API group; `TestNotificationSoundRoutesRequireAuth` locks unauthenticated
  POST/GET/DELETE to 401. Evidence: `server/app.ts:138-153`, `server/app.ts:220`,
  `server/routes/notification-sounds.ts:55-111`, `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:84-90`,
  `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:158-172`, `go_backend/internal/app/router_test.go:240`.
- The TS route group `/api/project-db` is mounted only under `/api/projects` for backup/import
  plus separately documented route file; Go mounts a small `/api/project-db` compatibility status
  group and also puts project backup/import under `/api/projects`. This is likely additive but
  should be kept documented. Evidence: `server/app.ts:249-250`,
  `server/routes/project-db.ts:17-46`, `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:136`,
  `go_backend/internal/systemroutes/handlers.go:118-125`,
  `go_backend/internal/projects/handlers.go:21-23`.

### P0

- **Resolved — Notification-sound mutation auth boundary now matches TS.** Current TS and Go both mount
  `/api/notification-sounds` under authenticated API routing. Keep `AUTH-NS-01` as a smoke row because
  the handler would fall back to an anonymous user if accidentally mounted publicly, but this is no longer
  an open P0 implementation blocker. Evidence: `server/app.ts:152-153`, `server/app.ts:220`,
  `server/routes/notification-sounds.ts:55-111`, `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:158-172`,
  `go_backend/internal/app/router_test.go:240`.
- **Resolved — Git-unavailable API contract guard matches TS shape.** TS returns a stable 503 payload with
  `code: "GIT_NOT_INSTALLED"` only for known Git-dependent routes when Git is absent. Current Go has the
  equivalent router-level guard and focused tests for guarded chapter/git routes plus non-Git over-block
  checks. Keep `API-GIT-01` as smoke coverage for Git-present/Git-missing fixtures. Evidence:
  `server/app.ts:155-203`, `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:209-233`,
  `go_backend/internal/app/route_contract_test.go:284`.

### P1

- **P1 — `/api/cline` auth/provider endpoint parity is narrowed but still partial.** Go registers the
  Cline route group and the TS-Go harness now covers status/balance plus safe cached/error-state
  routes for models, provider-cache miss, provider refresh miss, populated pool search/count,
  invalid enabled models, enabled-model add against a deterministic local cache, settings
  persisted-provider view, pasted OAuth callback credential import/free-model/user-info side effects,
  browser auth start plus localhost callback-server token import/free-model/user-info side effects,
  explicit user-info refresh, expired-token refresh, invalid-refresh cleanup, and balance flows against
  a local account fixture, offline fixture-backed positive provider refresh/cache persistence against the OpenRouter model shape,
  recommended/free model refresh/cache persistence against the Cline service shape, auth
  cancel/logout, and invalid callback. Remaining compatibility proof is mostly live upstream edge
  cases beyond the local fixture-backed credential/model paths. Evidence: `server/app.ts:225`,
  `server/routes/cline.ts:408-684`, `go_backend/internal/systemroutes/handlers.go:2097-2114`,
  `go_backend/testtools/ts-go-api-contract-diff.ts`.
- **P1 — `/api/nug` exact contract is narrowed but still partial.** Go registers NUG provider
  paths and the public OAuth callback, and the TS-Go harness now covers positive model refresh/cache,
  quota/cache, channels health, usage, usage-summary normalization, provider login, API-key creation,
  and OAuth start/callback success against local NUG fixtures. Login/API-key responses are locked to
  TS-compatible no implicit settings persistence; OAuth callback is locked to the TS persisted
  credential path with masked settings reads. Remaining unproven NUG parity is live upstream edge
  cases beyond the local fixture. Evidence: `server/app.ts:149-150`, `server/app.ts:228`,
  `server/routes/nug.ts:245-559`, `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:75`,
  `go_backend/internal/systemroutes/handlers.go:855-865`.
- **P1 — Degraded/stub-compatible responses may satisfy path existence but not TS behavior.**
  Gateway, update apply, benchmark execution, Kiro chat, and storage cleanup/database maintenance return fallback
  or unavailable payloads in Go where TS has real runtimes. Benchmark result/summary/compare reads are
  now explicitly health-described as admin-only read routes under `benchmark.results`, including limits,
  field names, compare diagnostics, summary `NOT_FOUND`, and `fullTSBenchmarkUIParity:false`; Wave 132
  rolls route/read/lifecycle/fake-Podman proof into `benchmark.replacementContract.replacementSafe:false`
  so those samples cannot be treated as full SWE-bench/runtime/UI parity. Wave 134 rolls storage scan/cleanup/database-maintenance proof into `storage.replacementContract.replacementSafe:false`, so fallback payloads and disabled VACUUM metadata cannot be treated as full storage-service parity. Wave 136 rolls stdio/per-call MCP proof into `mcp.replacementContract.replacementSafe:false`, so MCP routes and external tool metadata cannot be treated as persistent runtime or non-stdio transport parity. Kiro chat is now explicitly capability-gated
  as `providers.kiro.routes.chat=false` in Go health, matching its `FEATURE_DISABLED` handler.
  VNet is tracked in the Runtime P0 WebSocket row as an active Go relay-only runtime with UDP rendezvous
  disabled; Wave 85 exposes status/WS/message/limit fallback metadata, and API consumers must tolerate
  degraded/fallback payloads or the routes need full implementations. Evidence:
  `server/routes/gateway.ts:30-226`, `server/routes/vnet.ts:7`,
  `server/routes/update.ts:25-143`, `server/routes/benchmarks.ts:111-204`,
  `server/routes/kiro.ts:263-315`, `server/routes/storage.ts:13-155`,
  `go_backend/internal/systemroutes/handlers.go:501-584`,
  `go_backend/internal/systemroutes/handlers.go:648-790`,
  `go_backend/internal/systemroutes/handlers.go:1013-1024`,
  `go_backend/internal/misccompat/handlers.go:614-680`.
- **P1 — Chapter/narrator surface differs beyond path matching.** Go has many matching endpoints
  and some additive chapter endpoints, but response schemas and streaming/SSE semantics need
  domain/narrator verification for messages, compact, plan mode, fork/merge, review, container,
  and commit-file routes. Evidence: `server/routes/chapters.ts:44-493`,
  `server/routes/narrators.ts:261-3290`, `go_backend/internal/chapters/handlers.go:29-66`,
  `go_backend/internal/narrators/handlers.go:32-142`.

### P2

- **P2 — Additive Go routes should be documented as Go-only compatibility extensions.** Current
  Go adds `/api/exploration-groups`, chapter batch-fork/split/cherry-pick/dependency-status/
  sync-upstream, and `/api/project-db` status helpers without TS mounts. Evidence:
  `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:115`, `go_backend/internal/chapters/handlers.go:41-46`,
  `go_backend/internal/systemroutes/handlers.go:118-125`.
- **P2 — Error body consistency should be spot-tested.** TS global errors use `{ error, code }`
  for `AppError` and `{ error }` for unknown errors; Go's `httpx` helpers appear similar but route
  handlers sometimes write ad-hoc `{ok:false}`, `{success:false}`, or fallback payloads. Evidence:
  `server/app.ts:255-262`, `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:145-148`,
  `go_backend/internal/systemroutes/handlers.go:349-360`,
  `go_backend/internal/systemroutes/handlers.go:735-790`.
- **P2 — Health/version metadata differs.** TS reports `APP_VERSION` and `GIT_COMMIT`; Go reports
  `go-backend-dev` and empty commit unless env/config fills them. This is acceptable for a
  compatibility backend if frontend only displays it, but should be documented. Evidence:
  `server/app.ts:100-111`, `go_backend/internal/app/router.go:156-176`.

### Frontend-driven TS-vs-Go Contract Matrix

This matrix is the Goal 4 gate: each row must have either TS-vs-Go diff evidence, a stable Go
fallback, or an explicit disabled-by-design decision before Go can be promoted beyond compatibility
beta. It is derived from `frontend/lib/api/*.ts`, frontend hooks/components, Go route contract tests,
and three 2026-05-18 `miao:gpt-5.5` audits of core UI, realtime, and provider/browser surfaces.

Verification snapshot on 2026-05-18:

- `go test ./internal/app -run 'TestFrontendContractSmokeHTTPPaths|TestPhase0HealthCapabilitiesExposeKnownGoFallbacks|TestH02GitMissingGuardReturnsTSShapeOnlyForGitRoutes' -count=1`
- `go test ./internal/ws -run 'TestFrontendUseNarratorWSContractSubscribeCatchupPresenceAndEvents|TestFrontendUseTerminalWSContractSubscribeInputResizeAndFanout' -count=1`
- `go test ./internal/systemroutes -run 'TestUpdateDownloadWithoutURLReturnsTerminalErrorSSE|TestUpdateDownloadStreamsProgressAndCompleteSSE|TestUpdateDownloadRejectsSHA512Mismatch|TestUpdateVersionExposesManualOnlyContract|TestUpdateDirectoryCleanupAndApplyExposeUnsupportedContract' -count=1`
